## 6. Duplicates

Text compared: `subject + body`, lower-cased, runs of whitespace replaced by one space. Near-duplicates: MinHash (128 permutations, seed 42) on 5-character shingles, LSH threshold 0.8; each LSH candidate pair is kept only if its estimated Jaccard is >= 0.8. Clusters are the connected groups of those pairs, so exact duplicates are always in the same cluster. All files are clustered together, so a `cluster_id` can span files. Confirmed near pairs: 947.

`*_dup_rate` = 1 - (distinct groups / rows): the share of rows that are a repeat. `rows_in_*>1` = the share of rows that have at least one twin.

~~~~

*body:*
~~~~text
Dear Customer Support Team,\n\nI hope this message finds you well. I am currently facing a problem with Microsoft Excel formulas in my spreadsheet not computing accurately. Despite verifying the formulas multiple times, the results are either incorrect or not updating when I change the input values as expected.\n\nI have attempted basic troubleshooting methods, such as ensuring that automatic calculation mode is active and restarting the application, but the issue remains. This problem affects my ability to work efficiently since I depend heavily on precise calculations.
~~~~

*answer:*
~~~~text
Thank you for reaching out regarding the issue with Excel formulas not calculating properly. We understand how crucial accurate computations are and appreciate the troubleshooting steps you've already performed.\n\nSince you've confirmed that automatic calculation mode is enabled and you've restarted the application, the next step is to examine the spreadsheet for circular references, which can sometimes prevent formulas from updating correctly. To do this, go to the Formulas tab, select Error Checking, and then Circular References. If any are found, try resolving them accordingly.
~~~~

~~~~

Könnten Sie bitte detaillierte Informationen zu den Maßnahmen geben, die eingesetzt wurden, um die sichere Aufbewahrung, Übertragung und Zugriff auf medizinische Akten zu gewährleisten? *answer:*

Sehr geehrter [Name], wir möchten Ihnen für Ihre Besorgnis bezüglich der Sicherheit medizinischer Daten danken. Unsere Krankenhaus-IT-Systeme umfassen umfassende Sicherheitsprotokolle, um die Vertraulichkeit und Integrität der Patientendaten zu schützen. Wir halten uns an strengen Datenschutzrichtlinien und Prozeduren, um sicherzustellen, dass die Aufbewahrung, Übertragung und Zugriff auf medizinische Akten sicher sind. Die Maßnahmen umfassen Verschlüsselung, sichere Authentifizierung und Berechtigungen, sowie regelmäßige Audits und Überwachungen, um potenzielle Sicherheitslücken zu erkennen und zu verhindern. Unsere Vorgehensweise entspricht den relevanten Vorschriften und Branchenstandards in Bezug auf den Datenschutz. ## 9. Baselines (English and German apart)

Input text: `subject + body` (the answer is not used). Each file is modelled on its own because label sets differ between files. Split: `GroupShuffleSplit`, 20% of duplicate clusters to test, seed 42, grouped by the pooled `cluster_id`, so no cluster is on both sides (checked by an assert). (a) majority class of the training split; (b) TF-IDF (word 1-2 grams, sublinear tf, min_df 2) + logistic regression (lbfgs, max_iter 3000, default C=1).

## Appendix: reading sample

`reading_sample_100.csv`: 100 tickets, language ['en'], one row per duplicate cluster. Strata = version x text-length third (thirds of `subject + body` character length, computed on the English pool). Equal share per stratum, random inside each (seed 42). Rows from a file without a `version` column form their own stratum `none`. Labels and answers are in `reading_sample_key.csv`.
